# Chapter 6-f — Group D: C-7D

**Document:** `NH_MASTER-21_SYSTEM_BEHAVIOR_v0_1_CANDIDATE__CH06-f.md`  
**Status:** CANDIDATE  
**Source repository:** `nesgeva/NH-GOVERNANCE`  
**Source commit:** `6a7160ba688ba4e433a31899162815df7e2bab17`  

This piece covers Living State and world-model grounding, node/edge schemas, state currency, episode identity, protected boundaries, differentiated capacity, proposed continuity links, the proposed state-review relevance declaration, active-core membership and operation recovery/recordkeeping. It retains already delivered grounding, telling identity, Person-Box, B9 and shared Bundle 4 record/lifecycle atoms. Connections remain CH06-g; action possibilities, action-result confirmation and full authority CH07; complete privacy/relevance/LMAC and response records CH08; visual-world and simulation implementation CH10-e; side paths CH11. Exact empirical thresholds, time values, review-event authorization and technology remain explicit gaps.

Citation keys: V10 = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_MASTER-20_CORRECTED_v10.md`; MAP = `02_WORKING_MAP/NH_COMPLETE_DESIGN_AND_WIRING_MAP_v1_6_CANDIDATE.md`; DD = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_DECISION_DEFAULTS-S19_v2_2.md`; `04/` = `04_ACCEPTED_STANDALONE_DESIGNS/`; `05/` = `05_ACTIVE_CANDIDATE/`; NHD identifiers locate decisions in the pinned index and do not replace behavior citations.

### C-7D.17.9 — B6 operational-record active-cold lifecycle
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — The common Bundle 4 log lifecycle applied under the state/world consumer's own fixed declared versioned cooling rule. [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — New operational records, actual authorized use, valid new links, protection conditions and the declared rule. [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Every new log begins active through a lifecycle event. Keeps it active during unresolved/in-progress work, current-chain reference, pending recovery/retry/reconciliation/violation/correction, actual authorized use or a valid new link. Cooling requires both age under the rule and no genuine use/new links. Reactivation requires actual authorized use or a valid link to active work; no extra approval is added unless that link's governing rule requires it. The fixed cooling rule may use record category and operation/lifecycle status. A future rule change requires quantitative evidence, concrete examples and Ness approval before becoming operative; exact cooling time values remain calibration matters. [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — Append-only active/cold lifecycle events; the original log remains unchanged and exactly retrievable when authorized. [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Cool merely because protections momentarily disappear; use hidden scores; reactivate solely from retrieval, similarity, co-retrieval, ranking, time, confidence or repetition; change evidence strength, grounding, truth, authority, provenance or history. [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Unsafe cooling/reactivation eligibility preserves previous active/cold status and records failed evaluation; no silent cooling, reactivation, hiding, dropping or reprioritization. [04/NH_BUNDLE_4_LIVING_STATE_COMPUTED_VIEW_ACTION_AND_WORLD_MODEL_COMPLETION_v1_3_CANDIDATE.md §14]

## Scope, paths and source dispositions

C-7D receives permitted readings, stable telling identities, clashes, Ness responses and result evidence, plus Person-Box identity anchors. It is upstream of C-7M; no Computed View output enters state evidence. Existing incoming C-7K/C-7J/C-7L and Ness-anchor relationships and four C-7M consumer uses are reciprocated. Action possibilities and the result-return interface retain CH07-a/b ownership. Full relevance machinery and LMAC interfaces remain CH08-b/c, response events CH08-f, privacy enforcement CH08-a and visual-world/simulation mechanisms CH10-e. Path assembly remains CH11.

The eight V10 node/edge families retain DESIGNED headers. Accepted Bundle 4 supplies the later conceptual schemas, qualitative evidence rules and lifecycle detail, without claiming implementation or closing empirical counts/windows, technology or exact review-event authorization. The prior V10/Defaults/Companion open-slot wording is compared with these accepted completions; it is not used to erase them. Bundle 4's historical statement that A17/A19 remained open is scoped to that package: later accepted A17 and room-start rules are consumed at their own boundaries, while full simulation ownership stays later. No historical recovery-ledger row supplies behavior, and the excluded Bundle 2 foundation remains excluded.

Common record fields carry through every B6 schema. Previously delivered A31 labels, telling eligibility and identity, Ness's Person-Box, B9 values, operation_id, created_at, operational-log references and the shared Bundle 4 fourteen-field log-lifecycle schema retain their existing IDs. C-7M's existing log atoms express Bundle 4's shared contract; this piece states the B6 consumer's own fixed versioned cooling-rule ownership and current use, without creating duplicate generic event fields. State currentness, world active-core membership and operational-log active/cold status remain three independent dimensions.

Source discovery covered accepted/active files by C-7D, Living State Web, world model, state node, currentness, evidence-family and the proposed RM-LS-01 terms. The relevant accepted packages and navigation/intent matches are listed below. Shared-package placement is partial: Bundle 4 action records and wording remain CH07, and Bundle 2's other declarations retain their consumer owners. The active branch/simulation intent supplies no new state mechanism; framework and UE5 direction matches are scoped to future index/interface work and their non-authoritative presentation boundary. Source-local decision labels are not substituted for full NHD identifiers.

Earlier-piece findings remain unedited: CH03-c has two additional C-7D target rows stamped ACCEPTED although the canonical root is DESIGNED (C-READ.10.10 USED BY row 7 and C-READ.10.14 USED BY row 6). The already recorded CH06-d grouped USED BY defect remains open. Every USED BY row in the current piece names one place.

## Plain-gate and empty-box review

All fields and use rows were reviewed with adjacent boxes and their named source scopes. Direct Ness statements remain evidence rather than routine approval gates. Source-level conditions for several-sign inference, materiality, hypothesis support, optional summary, promotion, deactivation and cooling are explicit without invented execution machinery. Family counting preserves every underlying record; episode matching does not collapse evidence. Relationship perspective and identity anchors do not create an objective third-party profile. Currentness, membership and log status remain separate. Existing generic atoms are reused by ID, and the proposed qualifier accompanies the proposed mode/continuity/shared-rule references. Every current USED BY row has one place. Prior incoming source/consumer relations were checked against delivered files. Only the existing C-READ source is stamped BUILT. The one plain gate is an internal material-usefulness precondition, not a missing component reference. Required schema-field slots are outputs, not invented execution gates. Trigger/failure-class descriptions alone do not create additional authorization steps. The explicit recorded-reason, vocabulary-search, optional-summary and activation guards are named in their applicable boxes.

| Card | Plain gate justification |
|---|---|
| C-7D.7 — Counterfactual node | Material usefulness is the source-stated precondition for automatic internal counterfactual reasoning and has no separate named owner. The simulation-approval boundary separately names C-7P. |
